"use client";

import { fileIconInfo } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface FileIconProps {
  mime: string;
  name?: string;
  size?: number;
  className?: string;
}

export function FileIcon({ mime, name = "", size = 20, className }: FileIconProps) {
  const { Icon, color, bg } = fileIconInfo(mime, name);
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "grid place-items-center rounded-lg shrink-0",
        className,
      )}
      style={{
        width: size + 12,
        height: size + 12,
        background: bg,
      }}
    >
      <Icon size={size} style={{ color }} strokeWidth={2} />
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: src/components/yudit/ui/file-row.tsx =====
// src/components/yudit/ui/file-row.tsx
"use client";

import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { Eye, Share2, Download, Trash2, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import type { FileItem } from "@/lib/yudit/types";
import { formatBytes, formatDate } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { fetchFileBlob } from "@/lib/api/files";
import { FileIcon } from "./file-icon";
import { CategoryBadge } from "./category-badge";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface FileRowProps {
  file: FileItem;
  index?: number;
  showFolder?: boolean;
  alwaysShowActions?: boolean;
}

export function FileRow({
  file,
  index = 0,
  showFolder = false,
  alwaysShowActions = false,
}: FileRowProps) {
  const openPreview = useYudit((s) => s.openPreview);
  const openShare = useYudit((s) => s.openShare);
  const showConfirm = useYudit((s) => s.showConfirm);
  const deleteFile = useYudit((s) => s.deleteFile);
  const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState(false);

  const handleDelete = () => {
    showConfirm("Hapus file?", "File akan dihapus permanen dari penyimpanan.", () => {
      deleteFile(file.id);
    });
  };

  const handleDownload = async () => {
    setDownloading(true);
    const toastId = toast.loading(`Mengunduh ${file.name}…`);
    try {
      const blob = await fetchFileBlob(file.id, "download");
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url;
      a.download = file.name;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      a.remove();
      // Delay revoke
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);
      toast.success(`"${file.name}" berhasil diunduh`, { id: toastId });
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast.error(e?.detail || "Gagal download file", { id: toastId });
    } finally {
      setDownloading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.2, delay: Math.min(index * 0.015, 0.2) }}
      className={cn(
        "group grid items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-transparent px-3 py-2.5 transition-colors",
        "hover:border-[var(--y-border-soft)] hover:bg-[rgba(22,31,63,0.6)]",
      )}
      style={{ gridTemplateColumns: "44px 1fr 90px 150px 120px" }}
    >
      <button
        onClick={() => openPreview(file.id)}
        className="flex items-center justify-center"
        title="Preview"
      >
        <FileIcon mime={file.mime_type || ""} name={file.name} />
      </button>

      <button
        onClick={() => openPreview(file.id)}
        className="flex min-w-0 flex-col text-left"
        title={file.name}
      >
        <span className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-[var(--y-text)] group-hover:text-[var(--y-primary)] transition-colors">
          {file.name}
        </span>
        {showFolder && file.folder_name && (
          <span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
            📅 {file.folder_name}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      <div className="text-xs tabular-nums text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
        {formatBytes(file.size)}
      </div>

      <div className="min-w-0">
        <CategoryBadge category={file.category} />
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
        <div
          className={cn(
            "flex items-center gap-0.5",
            alwaysShowActions
              ? "opacity-100"
              : "opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity",
          )}
        >
          <IconBtn label="Preview" onClick={() => openPreview(file.id)}>
            <Eye size={14} />
          </IconBtn>
          <IconBtn label="Bagikan" onClick={() => openShare(file.id)}>
            <Share2 size={14} />
          </IconBtn>
          <IconBtn label="Download" onClick={handleDownload}>
            {downloading ? (
              <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <Download size={14} />
            )}
          </IconBtn>
          <IconBtn label="Hapus" danger onClick={handleDelete}>
            <Trash2 size={14} />
          </IconBtn>
        </div>
        <span className="text-[11px] tabular-nums text-[var(--y-text-mute)] group-hover:hidden pl-2">
          {formatDate(file.created_at)}
        </span>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function IconBtn({
  children,
  onClick,
  danger,
  label,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
  danger?: boolean;
  label: string;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={(e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        onClick?.();
      }}
      title={label}
      aria-label={label}
      className={cn(
        "grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-md text-[var(--y-text-dim)] transition-colors",
        "hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]",
        danger && "hover:bg-[rgba(239,68,68,0.15)] hover:text-[var(--y-danger)]",
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}


===== FILE: src/components/yudit/modals/preview-modal.tsx =====
// src/components/yudit/modals/preview-modal.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";
import { X, Download, Loader2, FileQuestion } from "lucide-react";

import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { formatBytes, formatDate, fileIconInfo, extOf } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import { fetchFileBlob } from "@/lib/api/files";
import { FileIcon } from "../ui/file-icon";
import { CategoryBadge } from "../ui/category-badge";

export function PreviewModal() {
  const previewFileId = useYudit((s) => s.previewFileId);
  const closePreview = useYudit((s) => s.closePreview);
  const files = useYudit((s) => s.files);
  const file = files.find((f) => f.id === previewFileId) || null;

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && previewFileId) closePreview();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handler);
  }, [previewFileId, closePreview]);

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {file && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[2000] flex flex-col"
          style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.9)", backdropFilter: "blur(12px)" }}
        >
          <div className="y-glass flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-white/10 px-5 py-3">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                {file.name}
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
                <span>{formatBytes(file.size)}</span>
                <span>•</span>
                <span>{formatDate(file.created_at)}</span>
                {file.category && (
                  <>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <CategoryBadge category={file.category} />
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <DownloadButton fileId={file.id} filename={file.name} />
              <button
                onClick={closePreview}
                className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-lg text-[var(--y-text-dim)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
                title="Tutup (Esc)"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center overflow-auto p-6">
            <PreviewBody key={file.id} file={file} />
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

// =====================================================
// DOWNLOAD BUTTON
// =====================================================
function DownloadButton({ fileId, filename }: { fileId: string; filename: string }) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleDownload = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const blob = await fetchFileBlob(fileId, "download");
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url;
      a.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      a.remove();
      // Delay revoke supaya browser sempat trigger download
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e?.detail || "Gagal download");
      setTimeout(() => setError(null), 3000);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleDownload}
      disabled={loading}
      className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] disabled:opacity-60"
      title={error || "Download"}
    >
      {loading ? <Loader2 size={15} className="animate-spin" /> : <Download size={15} />}
      <span className="hidden sm:inline">{error || "Download"}</span>
    </button>
  );
}

// =====================================================
// PREVIEW BODY — fetch blob
// =====================================================
function PreviewBody({
  file,
}: {
  file: { id: string; name: string; mime_type: string | null };
}) {
  const [blobUrl, setBlobUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    let url: string | null = null;

    (async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        const blob = await fetchFileBlob(file.id, "preview");
        url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        if (!cancelled) {
          setBlobUrl(url);
          setLoading(false);
        }
      } catch (e: any) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(e?.detail || "Gagal memuat preview");
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      // Jangan revoke terlalu cepat — biarkan browser pakai dulu
      if (url) setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url!), 500);
    };
  }, [file.id]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
        <Loader2 size={32} className="animate-spin text-[var(--y-primary)]" />
        <div className="text-sm">Memuat preview…</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !blobUrl) {
    return (
      <div className="text-center">
        <FileQuestion size={80} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-[var(--y-text-mute)]" />
        <div className="mb-1 text-base font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
          {error || "Preview tidak tersedia"}
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
          Klik "Download" untuk mengunduh file
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return <PreviewContent file={file} url={blobUrl} />;
}

function PreviewContent({
  file,
  url,
}: {
  file: { name: string; mime_type: string | null };
  url: string;
}) {
  const ext = extOf(file.name);
  const mime = file.mime_type || "";
  const info = fileIconInfo(mime, file.name);

  // Image
  if (
    mime.startsWith("image/") ||
    ["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "gif", "webp", "svg", "bmp"].includes(ext)
  ) {
    return (
      <motion.img
        initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
        src={url}
        alt={file.name}
        className="max-h-[80vh] max-w-[90vw] rounded-xl object-contain"
      />
    );
  }

  // Video
  if (mime.startsWith("video/") || ["mp4", "webm", "mov", "mkv"].includes(ext)) {
    return (
      <motion.video
        initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
        src={url}
        controls
        autoPlay
        className="aspect-video max-h-[80vh] w-full max-w-4xl rounded-xl bg-black"
      />
    );
  }

  // Audio
  if (mime.startsWith("audio/") || ["mp3", "wav", "flac", "m4a"].includes(ext)) {
    return (
      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        className="w-full max-w-xl"
      >
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-8">
          <div className="mb-4 flex justify-center">
            <info.Icon size={64} style={{ color: info.color }} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          </div>
          <audio src={url} controls className="w-full" />
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    );
  }

  // PDF
  if (ext === "pdf") {
    return (
      <iframe
        src={url}
        className="h-[80vh] w-full max-w-4xl rounded-xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-white"
        title={file.name}
      />
    );
  }

  // Text / code
  if (
    ["txt", "md", "json", "csv", "xml", "log", "py", "js", "ts", "html", "css"].includes(ext)
  ) {
    return <TextPreview url={url} name={file.name} />;
  }

  // Fallback
  return (
    <div className="text-center">
      <FileQuestion size={80} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-[var(--y-text-mute)]" />
      <div className="mb-1 text-base font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
        Preview tidak tersedia untuk tipe ini
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
        Klik "Download" untuk mengunduh file
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function TextPreview({ url, name }: { url: string; name: string }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState<string>("Memuat…");
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(url)
      .then((r) => r.text())
      .then((t) => {
        if (!cancelled) setText(t);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setText("Gagal memuat teks");
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [url]);

  return (
    <motion.pre
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      className="y-glow-soft max-h-full w-full max-w-3xl overflow-auto rounded-xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-5 font-mono text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-[var(--y-text)] whitespace-pre-wrap"
    >
      {`// ${name}\n\n${text}`}
    </motion.pre>
  );
}


===== FILE: src/components/yudit/modals/share-modal.tsx =====
// src/components/yudit/modals/share-modal.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  X,
  Link2,
  Copy,
  Check,
  ShieldCheck,
  Trash2,
  Clock,
  Eye,
  Loader2,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { formatBytes } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import { getShareUrl } from "@/lib/api/share";
import { FileIcon } from "../ui/file-icon";

export function ShareModal() {
  const shareFileId = useYudit((s) => s.shareFileId);
  const closeShare = useYudit((s) => s.closeShare);
  const files = useYudit((s) => s.files);
  const file = files.find((f) => f.id === shareFileId) || null;

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && shareFileId) closeShare();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handler);
  }, [shareFileId, closeShare]);

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {file && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[998] grid place-items-center p-5"
          style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)", backdropFilter: "blur(4px)" }}
          onClick={closeShare}
        >
          <motion.div
            initial={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.18 }}
            className="y-glass-strong y-glow-soft w-full max-w-[500px] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] p-6"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <ShareForm key={file.id} file={file} onClose={closeShare} />
          </motion.div>
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

function ShareForm({
  file,
  onClose,
}: {
  file: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    mime_type: string | null;
    size: number;
    is_public: boolean;
    share_token: string | null;
    share_views: number;
    share_expires_at?: string | null;
  };
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const createShare = useYudit((s) => s.createShare);
  const revokeShare = useYudit((s) => s.revokeShare);
  const showConfirm = useYudit((s) => s.showConfirm);

  const [expires, setExpires] = useState("168");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    setCreating(true);
    try {
      await createShare({
        expires_hours: expires ? parseInt(expires) : null,
        password: password.trim() || null,
      });
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = () => {
    if (!file.share_token) return;
    const url = getShareUrl(file.share_token);
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(url).catch(() => {});
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleRevoke = () => {
    showConfirm("Cabut share?", "Link yang sudah dibagikan tidak akan bisa diakses lagi.", async () => {
      await revokeShare(file.id);
      onClose();
    });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="mb-5 flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className="y-gradient-brand y-glow-primary grid h-11 w-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl">
          <Link2 size={20} className="text-white" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">Bagikan File</h3>
          <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-2">
            <FileIcon mime={file.mime_type || ""} name={file.name} size={16} />
            <span className="truncate text-xs text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
              {file.name} • {formatBytes(file.size)}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-lg text-[var(--y-text-mute)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
        >
          <X size={16} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {file.is_public && file.share_token ? (
        <div className="mb-4">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[rgba(16,185,129,0.3)] bg-[rgba(16,185,129,0.1)] px-3 py-2.5">
            <ShieldCheck size={16} className="shrink-0 text-[var(--y-success)]" />
            <span className="text-[13px] text-[#34d399]">
              File ini sudah dibagikan. Salin link di bawah.
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
            <Eye size={12} />
            <span>{file.share_views} views</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="y-focus flex-1 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 py-2.5 font-mono text-[12px] text-[var(--y-accent)]">
              {getShareUrl(file.share_token)}
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={handleCopy}
              className="inline-flex h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)]"
            >
              {copied ? (
                <Check size={15} className="text-[var(--y-success)]" />
              ) : (
                <Copy size={15} />
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
            <button
              onClick={handleRevoke}
              className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-[rgba(239,68,68,0.1)] px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-danger)] transition-colors hover:bg-[rgba(239,68,68,0.2)]"
            >
              <Trash2 size={14} />
              Cabut Share
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="mb-4 space-y-3">
            <ShareOption icon={<Clock size={15} />} label="Kedaluwarsa">
              <select
                value={expires}
                onChange={(e) => setExpires(e.target.value)}
                className="y-focus h-9 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text)] outline-none"
              >
                <option value="">Tidak kedaluwarsa</option>
                <option value="24">24 jam</option>
                <option value="168">7 hari</option>
                <option value="720">30 hari</option>
              </select>
            </ShareOption>
            <ShareOption icon={<ShieldCheck size={15} />} label="Password">
              <input
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Kosongkan jika tidak perlu"
                className="y-focus h-9 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text)] placeholder:text-[var(--y-text-mute)] outline-none"
              />
            </ShareOption>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <button
              onClick={handleCreate}
              disabled={creating}
              className="y-gradient-brand y-glow-primary inline-flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {creating ? <Loader2 size={15} className="animate-spin" /> : <Link2 size={15} />}
              Buat Link
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function ShareOption({
  icon,
  label,
  children,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
      <div className="flex w-[110px] shrink-0 items-center gap-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
        <span className="text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">{icon}</span>
        {label}
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
