// src/components/yudit/modals/category-manager-modal.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";
import { X, Plus, Trash2, Tags, Sparkles, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";

import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export function CategoryManagerModal() {
  const open = useYudit((s) => s.categoryManagerOpen);
  const setOpen = useYudit((s) => s.setCategoryManagerOpen);
  const defaultCats = useYudit((s) => s.defaultCategories);
  const customCats = useYudit((s) => s.customCategories);
  const addCustomCategory = useYudit((s) => s.addCustomCategory);
  const removeCustomCategory = useYudit((s) => s.removeCustomCategory);
  const showConfirm = useYudit((s) => s.showConfirm);

  const [icon, setIcon] = useState("📄");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [keywords, setKeywords] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && open) setOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handler);
  }, [open, setOpen]);

  const handleAdd = async () => {
    if (!name.trim()) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const kw = keywords
        .split(",")
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter(Boolean);
      await addCustomCategory(name.trim(), icon.trim() || "📄", kw);
      setName("");
      setKeywords("");
      setIcon("📄");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRemove = (id: string, name: string) => {
    showConfirm(
      `Hapus "${name}"?`,
      'File dengan kategori ini akan dipindah ke "Lainnya".',
      async () => {
        await removeCustomCategory(id);
      },
    );
  };

  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {open && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[998] grid place-items-center p-5"
          style={{ background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)", backdropFilter: "blur(4px)" }}
          onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
        >
          <motion.div
            initial={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ scale: 0.95, opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.18 }}
            className="y-glass-strong y-glow-soft flex max-h-[88vh] w-full max-w-[600px] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)]"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-[var(--y-border)] p-5">
              <div className="y-gradient-brand y-glow-primary grid h-10 w-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-xl">
                <Tags size={18} className="text-white" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">Kelola Kategori</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
                  Kategori bawaan sistem tidak bisa dihapus.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-lg text-[var(--y-text-mute)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
              >
                <X size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="max-h-[280px] overflow-y-auto p-4">
              <div className="mb-2 px-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                Kategori Bawaan
              </div>
              <div className="mb-4 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                {defaultCats.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="rounded-lg border border-dashed border-[var(--y-border)] py-3 text-center text-xs text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                    Belum dimuat
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  defaultCats.map((c) => (
                    <div
                      key={c.id || c.name}
                      className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 py-2"
                    >
                      <span className="text-lg">{c.icon}</span>
                      <span className="flex-1 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                        {c.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="rounded-full bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                        Bawaan
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="mb-2 px-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                Kategori Custom
              </div>
              {customCats.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="rounded-lg border border-dashed border-[var(--y-border)] py-6 text-center text-xs text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                  Belum ada kategori custom. Tambahkan di bawah.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
                  {customCats.map((c) => (
                    <div
                      key={c.id}
                      className="group flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 py-2"
                    >
                      <span className="text-lg">{c.icon}</span>
                      <span className="flex-1 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                        {c.name}
                      </span>
                      {c.keywords.length > 0 && (
                        <span className="hidden text-[11px] text-[var(--y-text-mute)] sm:inline">
                          {c.keywords.join(", ")}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleRemove(c.id, c.name)}
                        className="grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-md text-[var(--y-text-mute)] transition-colors hover:bg-[rgba(239,68,68,0.15)] hover:text-[var(--y-danger)]"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="border-t border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[rgba(11,16,32,0.4)] p-4">
              <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                <Sparkles size={14} className="text-[var(--y-primary)]" />
                Tambah Kategori Baru
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-[60px_1fr] gap-2.5">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">Icon</label>
                  <input
                    value={icon}
                    onChange={(e) => setIcon(e.target.value)}
                    maxLength={2}
                    className="y-focus h-9 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] text-center text-lg outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">Nama</label>
                  <input
                    value={name}
                    onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Contoh: Laporan Bulanan"
                    className="y-focus h-9 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text)] placeholder:text-[var(--y-text-mute)] outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2.5 flex flex-col gap-1">
                <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                  Keywords (pisahkan koma)
                </label>
                <input
                  value={keywords}
                  onChange={(e) => setKeywords(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="laporan, bulanan, report"
                  className="y-focus h-9 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text)] placeholder:text-[var(--y-text-mute)] outline-none"
                />
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleAdd}
                disabled={!name.trim() || saving}
                className={cn(
                  "y-gradient-brand mt-3 inline-flex h-10 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg text-[13px] font-semibold text-white transition-all",
                  "hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:hover:translate-y-0",
                )}
              >
                {saving ? <Loader2 size={15} className="animate-spin" /> : <Plus size={15} />}
                Tambah Kategori
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}


===== FILE: src/components/yudit/views/files-view.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { Search, SlidersHorizontal, Tags, RefreshCw, X, Inbox } from "lucide-react";
import { useYudit, categoryCounts, availableYears } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { CATEGORY_META, getCategoryMeta, FILE_TYPES, MONTHS_ID } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import type { FileItem, Filters } from "@/lib/yudit/types";
import { FileRow } from "../ui/file-row";
import { Dropzone } from "../ui/dropzone";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export function FilesView() {
  const files = useYudit((s) => s.files);
  const customCategories = useYudit((s) => s.customCategories);
  const currentCategory = useYudit((s) => s.currentCategory);
  const setCategory = useYudit((s) => s.setCategory);
  const searchQuery = useYudit((s) => s.searchQuery);
  const setSearch = useYudit((s) => s.setSearch);
  const filters = useYudit((s) => s.filters);
  const setFilters = useYudit((s) => s.setFilters);
  const resetFilters = useYudit((s) => s.resetFilters);
  const filterPanelOpen = useYudit((s) => s.filterPanelOpen);
  const setFilterPanelOpen = useYudit((s) => s.setFilterPanelOpen);
  const setCategoryManagerOpen = useYudit((s) => s.setCategoryManagerOpen);

  const counts = useMemo(() => categoryCounts(files), [files]);
  const years = useMemo(() => availableYears(files), [files]);
  const totalCount = files.length;

  // Filtered files
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let list = [...files];
    if (currentCategory !== "all") {
      list = list.filter((f) => f.category === currentCategory);
    }
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q) {
      list = list.filter((f) => f.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    }
    const f = filters;
    if (f.year) list = list.filter((x) => x.year === f.year);
    if (f.month) list = list.filter((x) => x.month === f.month);
    if (f.date_from)
      list = list.filter((x) => new Date(x.created_at) >= new Date(f.date_from!));
    if (f.date_to)
      list = list.filter((x) => new Date(x.created_at) <= new Date(f.date_to! + "T23:59:59"));
    if (f.file_type) {
      list = list.filter((x) => {
        const ext = (x.name.split(".").pop() || "").toLowerCase();
        return ext === f.file_type;
      });
    }
    // sort
    const sortBy = f.sort_by || "created_at";
    const sortOrder = f.sort_order || "desc";
    list.sort((a, b) => {
      let cmp = 0;
      if (sortBy === "name") cmp = a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else if (sortBy === "size") cmp = a.size - b.size;
      else cmp = new Date(a.created_at).getTime() - new Date(b.created_at).getTime();
      return sortOrder === "asc" ? cmp : -cmp;
    });
    return list;
  }, [files, currentCategory, searchQuery, filters]);

  return (
    <div className="y-anim-fade">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[var(--y-text)]">My Files</h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            {filtered.length} file{filtered.length !== 1 && "s"}
            {currentCategory !== "all" && ` • ${currentCategory}`}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Search */}
      <div className="relative mb-3">
        <Search
          size={16}
          className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--y-text-mute)]"
        />
        <input
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Cari nama file…"
          className="y-focus h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] pl-10 pr-10 text-sm text-[var(--y-text)] placeholder:text-[var(--y-text-mute)] transition-all"
        />
        {searchQuery && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setSearch("")}
            className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--y-text-mute)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Toolbar */}
      <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <ToolbarBtn
          active={filterPanelOpen}
          onClick={() => setFilterPanelOpen(!filterPanelOpen)}
        >
          <SlidersHorizontal size={14} />
          Filter Lanjutan
          {Object.keys(filters).length > 0 && (
            <span className="ml-1 rounded-full bg-[var(--y-primary)] px-1.5 text-[10px] text-white">
              {Object.keys(filters).filter((k) => k !== "sort_by" && k !== "sort_order").length}
            </span>
          )}
        </ToolbarBtn>
        <ToolbarBtn onClick={() => setCategoryManagerOpen(true)}>
          <Tags size={14} />
          Kelola Kategori
        </ToolbarBtn>
        <ToolbarBtn
          onClick={() => {
            setFilters({});
            setSearch("");
            setCategory("all");
          }}
        >
          <RefreshCw size={14} />
          Refresh
        </ToolbarBtn>
      </div>

      {/* Filter Panel */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {filterPanelOpen && (
          <FilterPanel
            years={years}
            current={filters}
            onApply={(f) => {
              setFilters(f);
              setFilterPanelOpen(false);
            }}
            onReset={() => {
              resetFilters();
            }}
          />
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Active filter badges */}
      <ActiveFilters
        filters={filters}
        onRemove={(key) => {
          const next = { ...filters };
          delete (next as Record<string, unknown>)[key];
          setFilters(next);
        }}
        onReset={resetFilters}
      />

      {/* Category tabs */}
      <CategoryTabs
        counts={counts}
        current={currentCategory}
        onSelect={setCategory}
        customCats={customCategories.map((c) => ({ name: c.name, icon: c.icon }))}
      />

      {/* Dropzone */}
      <Dropzone />

      {/* File list */}
      <FileList files={filtered} emptyCategory={currentCategory} />
    </div>
  );
}

function ToolbarBtn({
  children,
  active,
  onClick,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  active?: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border px-3 text-xs font-semibold transition-all",
        active
          ? "border-[var(--y-primary)] bg-[var(--y-primary)] text-white"
          : "border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] text-[var(--y-text-dim)] hover:border-[var(--y-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]",
      )}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function FilterPanel({
  years,
  current,
  onApply,
  onReset,
}: {
  years: string[];
  current: Filters;
  onApply: (f: Filters) => void;
  onReset: () => void;
}) {
  const [year, setYear] = useState(current.year ? String(current.year) : "");
  const [month, setMonth] = useState(current.month ? String(current.month) : "");
  const [dateFrom, setDateFrom] = useState(current.date_from || "");
  const [dateTo, setDateTo] = useState(current.date_to || "");
  const [fileType, setFileType] = useState(current.file_type || "");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState(
    current.sort_by && current.sort_order
      ? `${current.sort_by}:${current.sort_order}`
      : "created_at:desc",
  );

  const apply = () => {
    const f: Filters = {};
    if (year) f.year = parseInt(year);
    if (month) f.month = parseInt(month);
    if (dateFrom) f.date_from = dateFrom;
    if (dateTo) f.date_to = dateTo;
    if (fileType) f.file_type = fileType;
    if (sort) {
      const [by, order] = sort.split(":");
      f.sort_by = by as Filters["sort_by"];
      f.sort_order = order as Filters["sort_order"];
    }
    onApply(f);
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, height: "auto" }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
      className="mb-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)]"
    >
      <div className="p-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <FilterGroup label="Tahun">
            <FilterSelect value={year} onChange={setYear}>
              <option value="">Semua Tahun</option>
              {years.map((y) => (
                <option key={y} value={y}>
                  {y}
                </option>
              ))}
            </FilterSelect>
          </FilterGroup>
          <FilterGroup label="Bulan">
            <FilterSelect value={month} onChange={setMonth}>
              <option value="">Semua Bulan</option>
              {MONTHS_ID.map((m, i) => (
                <option key={i} value={String(i + 1)}>
                  {m}
                </option>
              ))}
            </FilterSelect>
          </FilterGroup>
          <FilterGroup label="Tipe File">
            <FilterSelect value={fileType} onChange={setFileType}>
              <option value="">Semua Tipe</option>
              {FILE_TYPES.map((t) => (
                <option key={t.value} value={t.value}>
                  {t.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </FilterSelect>
          </FilterGroup>
          <FilterGroup label="Dari Tanggal">
            <FilterInput type="date" value={dateFrom} onChange={setDateFrom} />
          </FilterGroup>
          <FilterGroup label="Sampai Tanggal">
            <FilterInput type="date" value={dateTo} onChange={setDateTo} />
          </FilterGroup>
          <FilterGroup label="Urutkan">
            <FilterSelect value={sort} onChange={setSort}>
              <option value="created_at:desc">Terbaru</option>
              <option value="created_at:asc">Terlama</option>
              <option value="name:asc">Nama A-Z</option>
              <option value="name:desc">Nama Z-A</option>
              <option value="size:desc">Ukuran Terbesar</option>
              <option value="size:asc">Ukuran Terkecil</option>
            </FilterSelect>
          </FilterGroup>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-[var(--y-border-soft)] pt-3">
          <button
            onClick={onReset}
            className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
          >
            Reset
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={apply}
            className="y-gradient-brand inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white"
          >
            Terapkan
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function FilterGroup({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
      <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
        {label}
      </label>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function FilterSelect({
  value,
  onChange,
  children,
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <select
      value={value}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
      className="y-focus h-9 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text)] outline-none"
    >
      {children}
    </select>
  );
}

function FilterInput({
  type,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  type: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <input
      type={type}
      value={value}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
      className="y-focus h-9 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text)] outline-none [color-scheme:dark]"
    />
  );
}

function ActiveFilters({
  filters,
  onRemove,
  onReset,
}: {
  filters: Filters;
  onRemove: (key: string) => void;
  onReset: () => void;
}) {
  const items: { key: string; label: string }[] = [];
  if (filters.year) items.push({ key: "year", label: `Tahun: ${filters.year}` });
  if (filters.month)
    items.push({ key: "month", label: `Bulan: ${MONTHS_ID[filters.month - 1]}` });
  if (filters.date_from) items.push({ key: "date_from", label: `Dari: ${filters.date_from}` });
  if (filters.date_to) items.push({ key: "date_to", label: `Sampai: ${filters.date_to}` });
  if (filters.file_type)
    items.push({ key: "file_type", label: `Tipe: ${filters.file_type.toUpperCase()}` });

  if (items.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
      {items.map((it) => (
        <div
          key={it.key}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--y-primary)] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-white"
        >
          {it.label}
          <button
            onClick={() => onRemove(it.key)}
            className="grid h-4 w-4 place-items-center rounded-full hover:bg-white/20"
          >
            <X size={11} />
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
      <button
        onClick={onReset}
        className="rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
      >
        Hapus Semua
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function CategoryTabs({
  counts,
  current,
  onSelect,
  customCats,
}: {
  counts: Record<string, number>;
  current: string;
  onSelect: (cat: string) => void;
  customCats: { name: string; icon: string }[];
}) {
  const totalCount = Object.values(counts).reduce((s, n) => s + n, 0);

  const tabs: { name: string; icon: string; count: number }[] = [];
  // custom first
  customCats.forEach((c) => {
    const cnt = counts[c.name] || 0;
    if (cnt > 0) tabs.push({ name: c.name, icon: c.icon, count: cnt });
  });
  // built-in priority order
  const PRIORITY = Object.keys(CATEGORY_META);
  PRIORITY.forEach((name) => {
    const cnt = counts[name] || 0;
    if (cnt > 0) {
      const meta = getCategoryMeta(name, customCats);
      tabs.push({ name, icon: meta.icon, count: cnt });
    }
  });

  return (
    <div className="mb-4 flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1.5 [scrollbar-width:thin]">
      <CatTab
        active={current === "all"}
        icon="📂"
        label="Semua"
        count={totalCount}
        onClick={() => onSelect("all")}
      />
      {tabs.map((t) => (
        <CatTab
          key={t.name}
          active={current === t.name}
          icon={t.icon}
          label={t.name}
          count={t.count}
          onClick={() => onSelect(t.name)}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function CatTab({
  active,
  icon,
  label,
  count,
  onClick,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  icon: string;
  label: string;
  count: number;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-all",
        active
          ? "border-transparent bg-gradient-to-br from-[var(--y-primary)] to-[#8b5cf6] text-white y-glow-primary"
          : "border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] text-[var(--y-text-dim)] hover:border-[var(--y-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]",
      )}
    >
      <span>{icon}</span>
      <span className="max-w-[140px] truncate">{label}</span>
      <span
        className={cn(
          "rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums",
          active ? "bg-white/25" : "bg-white/10",
        )}
      >
        {count}
      </span>
    </button>
  );
}

function FileList({
  files,
  emptyCategory,
}: {
  files: FileItem[];
  emptyCategory: string;
}) {
  if (files.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="grid place-items-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-[var(--y-border)] py-16 text-center">
        <div className="grid h-16 w-16 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)]">
          <Inbox size={28} className="text-[var(--y-text-mute)]" />
        </div>
        <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
          {emptyCategory !== "all"
            ? `Belum ada file kategori "${emptyCategory}"`
            : "Belum ada file"}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
          Upload file untuk memulai
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Header row
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border-soft)] bg-[rgba(17,24,53,0.4)] p-2">
      <div
        className="hidden items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[var(--y-text-mute)] sm:grid"
        style={{ gridTemplateColumns: "44px 1fr 90px 150px 120px" }}
      >
        <div className="text-center">Tipe</div>
        <div>Nama</div>
        <div>Ukuran</div>
        <div>Kategori</div>
        <div className="text-right">Aksi</div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
        {files.map((f, i) => (
          <FileRow key={f.id} file={f} index={i} showFolder={false} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: src/components/yudit/views/stats-view.tsx =====
// src/components/yudit/views/stats-view.tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  Files,
  HardDrive,
  CalendarRange,
  FileBox,
  RefreshCw,
  TrendingUp,
  Loader2,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { getStats, type StatsResponse } from "@/lib/api/files";
import { formatBytes, MONTHS_SHORT, getCategoryMeta } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";

export function StatsView() {
  const customCats = useYudit((s) => s.customCategories);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<StatsResponse | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const load = useMemo(
    () => async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const s = await getStats();
        setStats(s);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load]);

  if (loading || !stats) {
    return (
      <div className="grid place-items-center py-20">
        <Loader2 size={28} className="animate-spin text-[var(--y-primary)]" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const cards = [
    {
      label: "Total File",
      value: String(stats.total_files),
      sub: "Semua file terupload",
      Icon: Files,
      color: "#6366f1",
      bg: "rgba(99,102,241,0.12)",
    },
    {
      label: "Total Ukuran",
      value: formatBytes(stats.total_size),
      sub: "Kapasitas terpakai",
      Icon: HardDrive,
      color: "#22d3ee",
      bg: "rgba(34,211,238,0.12)",
    },
    {
      label: "Tahun Aktif",
      value: String(Object.keys(stats.by_year).length),
      sub: "Tahun dengan file",
      Icon: CalendarRange,
      color: "#10b981",
      bg: "rgba(16,185,129,0.12)",
    },
    {
      label: "Tipe File",
      value: String(Object.keys(stats.by_type).length),
      sub: "Format berbeda",
      Icon: FileBox,
      color: "#f59e0b",
      bg: "rgba(245,158,11,0.12)",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="y-anim-fade">
      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[var(--y-text)]">
            Statistik &amp; Analytics
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            Overview penggunaan storage &amp; dokumen
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={load}
          className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)] transition-all hover:border-[var(--y-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
        >
          <RefreshCw size={15} />
          Refresh
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {cards.map((c, i) => (
          <motion.div
            key={c.label}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
            className="group rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-4 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(99,102,241,0.4)]"
          >
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                {c.label}
              </span>
              <div
                className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-lg transition-transform group-hover:scale-110"
                style={{ background: c.bg }}
              >
                <c.Icon size={15} style={{ color: c.color }} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="y-gradient-text mb-1 text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums">
              {c.value}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">{c.sub}</div>
          </motion.div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <ChartCard
          title="Distribusi Per Bulan"
          icon="📅"
          entries={Object.entries(stats.by_month)
            .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
            .slice(-8)}
          labelFn={(k) => {
            const [y, m] = k.split("-");
            return `${MONTHS_SHORT[parseInt(m) - 1]} ${y}`;
          }}
        />
        <ChartCard
          title="Distribusi Per Kategori"
          icon="🏷️"
          entries={Object.entries(stats.by_category).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])}
          labelFn={(k) => k}
          colored
          customCats={customCats.map((c) => ({ name: c.name, icon: c.icon }))}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4">
        <ChartCard
          title="Distribusi Per Tipe File"
          icon="📄"
          entries={Object.entries(stats.by_type).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])}
          labelFn={(k) => k.toUpperCase()}
          full
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ChartCard({
  title,
  icon,
  entries,
  labelFn,
  colored,
  customCats,
  full,
}: {
  title: string;
  icon: string;
  entries: [string, number][];
  labelFn: (k: string) => string;
  colored?: boolean;
  customCats?: { name: string; icon: string }[];
  full?: boolean;
}) {
  const max = entries.length ? Math.max(...entries.map((e) => e[1])) : 1;

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      className="rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-5"
    >
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-base">{icon}</span>
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--y-text)]">{title}</h3>
        <span className="ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
          <TrendingUp size={12} />
          {entries.length} item
        </span>
      </div>
      {entries.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="py-8 text-center text-xs text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
          Belum ada data
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
          {entries.slice(0, 12).map(([key, count], i) => {
            const pct = max > 0 ? (count / max) * 100 : 0;
            const label = labelFn(key);
            const meta = colored ? getCategoryMeta(key, customCats || []) : null;
            return (
              <motion.div
                key={key}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: i * 0.04 }}
                className="flex items-center gap-3"
              >
                <div
                  className="w-32 shrink-0 truncate text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)]"
                  title={label}
                >
                  {meta && <span className="mr-1">{meta.icon}</span>}
                  {label}
                </div>
                <div className="relative h-6 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-md bg-[var(--y-bg)]">
                  <motion.div
                    className="flex h-full items-center justify-end rounded-md pr-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-white"
                    style={{
                      background: meta
                        ? `linear-gradient(90deg, ${meta.color}, ${meta.color}cc)`
                        : "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-primary), var(--y-accent))",
                    }}
                    initial={{ width: 0 }}
                    animate={{ width: `${Math.max(pct, 10)}%` }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: i * 0.04, ease: "easeOut" }}
                  >
                    {count}
                  </motion.div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </motion.div>
  );
}


===== FILE: src/components/yudit/views/login-view.tsx =====
// src/components/yudit/views/login-view.tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { Cloud, Mail, Lock, User as UserIcon, ArrowRight, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type Tab = "login" | "register";

export function LoginView() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("login");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const login = useYudit((s) => s.login);
  const register = useYudit((s) => s.register);

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await login(email, password);
    } catch {
      // error sudah di-toast
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRegister = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password || !username) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await register(email, username, password);
    } catch {
      // error sudah di-toast
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="yudit-ambient relative grid min-h-screen place-items-center overflow-hidden p-5">
      <div
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-20 top-10 h-72 w-72 rounded-full opacity-30 blur-3xl"
        style={{ background: "radial-gradient(circle, var(--y-primary), transparent 70%)" }}
      />
      <div
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -right-20 bottom-10 h-72 w-72 rounded-full opacity-25 blur-3xl"
        style={{ background: "radial-gradient(circle, var(--y-accent), transparent 70%)" }}
      />

      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        transition={{ duration: 0.4 }}
        className="y-glass-strong y-glow-soft relative z-10 w-full max-w-[420px] rounded-3xl border border-[var(--y-border)] p-8"
      >
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center">
          <motion.div
            initial={{ scale: 0.8, rotate: -8 }}
            animate={{ scale: 1, rotate: 0 }}
            transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 200, damping: 15, delay: 0.1 }}
            className="y-gradient-brand y-glow-primary mb-4 grid h-16 w-16 place-items-center rounded-2xl"
          >
            <Cloud size={32} className="text-white" fill="currentColor" />
          </motion.div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[var(--y-text)]">
            Yudit<span className="y-gradient-text">Cloud</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-center text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            Satu cloud virtual, banyak Google Drive di belakangnya
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-6 flex gap-1 rounded-xl bg-[var(--y-bg)] p-1">
          {(["login", "register"] as Tab[]).map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTab(t)}
              className="relative flex-1 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors"
              style={{ color: tab === t ? "var(--y-text)" : "var(--y-text-dim)" }}
            >
              {tab === t && (
                <motion.div
                  layoutId="auth-tab"
                  className="absolute inset-0 rounded-lg bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] shadow-sm"
                  style={{ border: "1px solid var(--y-border-soft)" }}
                  transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 350, damping: 30 }}
                />
              )}
              <span className="relative z-10">
                {t === "login" ? "Masuk" : "Daftar"}
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          {tab === "login" ? (
            <motion.form
              key="login"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -8 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 8 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
              onSubmit={handleLogin}
              className="space-y-4"
            >
              <Field
                icon={<Mail size={16} />}
                label="Email"
                type="email"
                placeholder="kamu@example.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={setEmail}
                autoComplete="email"
              />
              <Field
                icon={<Lock size={16} />}
                label="Password"
                type="password"
                placeholder="••••••••"
                value={password}
                onChange={setPassword}
                autoComplete="current-password"
              />
              <SubmitButton label="Masuk" loading={loading} />
            </motion.form>
          ) : (
            <motion.form
              key="register"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 8 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -8 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
              onSubmit={handleRegister}
              className="space-y-4"
            >
              <Field
                icon={<UserIcon size={16} />}
                label="Username"
                type="text"
                placeholder="username"
                value={username}
                onChange={setUsername}
                autoComplete="username"
              />
              <Field
                icon={<Mail size={16} />}
                label="Email"
                type="email"
                placeholder="kamu@example.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={setEmail}
                autoComplete="email"
              />
              <Field
                icon={<Lock size={16} />}
                label="Password"
                type="password"
                placeholder="min 6 karakter"
                value={password}
                onChange={setPassword}
                autoComplete="new-password"
              />
              <SubmitButton label="Daftar" loading={loading} />
            </motion.form>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({
  icon,
  label,
  type,
  placeholder,
  value,
  onChange,
  autoComplete,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  type: string;
  placeholder?: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (v: string) => void;
  autoComplete?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
        {label}
      </label>
      <div className="group relative">
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--y-text-mute)] transition-colors group-focus-within:text-[var(--y-primary)]">
          {icon}
        </div>
        <input
          type={type}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
          required
          autoComplete={autoComplete}
          className="y-focus h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-[var(--y-text)] placeholder:text-[var(--y-text-mute)] transition-all"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SubmitButton({ label, loading }: { label: string; loading: boolean }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="submit"
      disabled={loading}
      className={cn(
        "y-gradient-brand y-glow-primary group relative flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 overflow-hidden rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-all",
        "hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:opacity-70 disabled:hover:translate-y-0",
      )}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />
      ) : (
        <>
          <span className="relative z-10">{label}</span>
          <ArrowRight
            size={16}
            className="relative z-10 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5"
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/20 to-transparent transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:translate-x-full" />
        </>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}


===== FILE: src/components/yudit/views/monthly-view.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { CalendarDays, ChevronRight, ChevronsUpDown, Search, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useYudit, groupByFolder } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { MONTHS_ID, formatBytes } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import type { FileItem } from "@/lib/yudit/types";
import { FileRow } from "../ui/file-row";

export function MonthlyView() {
  const files = useYudit((s) => s.files);
  const search = useYudit((s) => s.monthlySearchQuery);
  const setSearch = useYudit((s) => s.setMonthlySearch);
  const expanded = useYudit((s) => s.expandedMonthly);
  const toggle = useYudit((s) => s.toggleMonthly);
  const collapseAll = useYudit((s) => s.collapseAllMonthly);
  const expandAll = useYudit((s) => s.expandAllMonthly);

  const grouped = useMemo(() => groupByFolder(files), [files]);

  const sortedKeys = useMemo(() => {
    const parseKey = (s: string) => {
      if (s.includes("Tanpa Folder")) return 0;
      const cleaned = s.replace("📅 ", "");
      const parts = cleaned.split(" ");
      const m = parts[0];
      const y = parseInt(parts[1] || "0");
      const mi = MONTHS_ID.indexOf(m);
      return y * 100 + (mi >= 0 ? mi + 1 : 0);
    };
    return Object.keys(grouped).sort((a, b) => parseKey(b) - parseKey(a));
  }, [grouped]);

  const filteredKeys = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return sortedKeys;
    return sortedKeys.filter((k) => {
      if (k.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return true;
      return grouped[k].some((f) => f.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    });
  }, [sortedKeys, grouped, search]);

  const totalFiles = filteredKeys.reduce(
    (s, k) => s + grouped[k].filter((f) => matchSearch(f.name, k, search)).length,
    0,
  );

  const anyExpanded = Object.values(expanded).some(Boolean);

  return (
    <div className="y-anim-fade">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[var(--y-text)]">Arsip Per Bulan</h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            {filteredKeys.length} folder • {totalFiles} file
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => (anyExpanded ? collapseAll() : expandAll())}
          className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)] transition-all hover:border-[var(--y-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
        >
          <ChevronsUpDown size={15} />
          {anyExpanded ? "Lipat Semua" : "Buka Semua"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Search */}
      <div className="relative mb-4">
        <Search
          size={16}
          className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--y-text-mute)]"
        />
        <input
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Cari di semua folder bulanan…"
          className="y-focus h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] pl-10 pr-10 text-sm text-[var(--y-text)] placeholder:text-[var(--y-text-mute)] transition-all"
        />
        {search && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setSearch("")}
            className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--y-text-mute)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)]"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Groups */}
      {filteredKeys.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState search={search} />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {filteredKeys.map((key, idx) => {
            const allFiles = grouped[key];
            const files = search
              ? allFiles.filter(
                  (f) =>
                    f.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
                    key.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()),
                )
              : allFiles;
            if (files.length === 0) return null;
            const totalSize = files.reduce((s, f) => s + f.size, 0);
            const isOpen = expanded[key] ?? (idx === 0 && !search);
            return (
              <MonthlyGroup
                key={key}
                name={key}
                count={files.length}
                totalSize={totalSize}
                isOpen={!!isOpen}
                onToggle={() => toggle(key)}
                files={files}
              />
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function matchSearch(name: string, folder: string, q: string) {
  if (!q) return true;
  const ql = q.toLowerCase();
  return name.toLowerCase().includes(ql) || folder.toLowerCase().includes(ql);
}

function MonthlyGroup({
  name,
  count,
  totalSize,
  isOpen,
  onToggle,
  files,
}: {
  name: string;
  count: number;
  totalSize: number;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
  files: FileItem[];
}) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      layout
      className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] transition-colors hover:border-[rgba(99,102,241,0.3)]"
    >
      <button
        onClick={onToggle}
        className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-b border-[var(--y-border-soft)] px-4 py-3.5 text-left transition-colors hover:bg-[rgba(99,102,241,0.06)]"
        style={{
          background: isOpen
            ? "linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(99,102,241,0.1), transparent)"
            : undefined,
        }}
      >
        <motion.span animate={{ rotate: isOpen ? 90 : 0 }} transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}>
          <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-[var(--y-text-mute)]" />
        </motion.span>
        <div className="grid h-9 w-9 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[rgba(99,102,241,0.15)]">
          <CalendarDays size={17} className="text-[var(--y-primary)]" />
        </div>
        <span className="flex-1 text-[15px] font-bold text-[var(--y-text)]">{name}</span>
        <span className="rounded-full border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg)] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)] tabular-nums">
          {count} file • {formatBytes(totalSize)}
        </span>
      </button>
      <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
        {isOpen && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.25, ease: "easeInOut" }}
            className="overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 p-2">
              {files.map((f, i) => (
                <FileRow key={f.id} file={f} index={i} showFolder={false} alwaysShowActions />
              ))}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function EmptyState({ search }: { search: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid place-items-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-[var(--y-border)] py-16 text-center">
      <div className="grid h-16 w-16 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)]">
        <CalendarDays size={28} className="text-[var(--y-text-mute)]" />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
        {search ? "Tidak ada hasil" : "Belum ada arsip bulanan"}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
        {search
          ? `Pencarian "${search}" tidak ditemukan`
          : "Upload file untuk mulai mengorganisir per bulan"}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: src/components/yudit/views/drives-view.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  Plus, Trash2, Mail, CheckCircle2, Loader2, RefreshCw,
} from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { formatBytes, formatDate } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import { connectGoogleDrive } from "@/lib/api/oauth";
import { forceRefreshQuota } from "@/lib/api/drives";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

// Batas opsional — None = unlimited
const MAX_DRIVES: number | null = null;


export function DrivesView() {
  const drives = useYudit((s) => s.drives);
  const pool = useYudit((s) => s.pool);
  const removeDrive = useYudit((s) => s.removeDrive);
  const fetchDrives = useYudit((s) => s.fetchDrives);
  const showConfirm = useYudit((s) => s.showConfirm);

  const [connecting, setConnecting] = useState(false);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);

  // Cek apakah masih boleh connect
  const canConnect =
    MAX_DRIVES === null || drives.length < MAX_DRIVES;

  const handleConnect = async () => {
    if (!canConnect) {
      toast.error(`Maksimal ${MAX_DRIVES} akun Google Drive`);
      return;
    }
    setConnecting(true);
    try {
      const res = await connectGoogleDrive();
      if (res.ok) {
        await fetchDrives();
        toast.success("Akun Google Drive terhubung");
      } else if (res.error === "duplicate_account") {
        toast.error("Akun ini sudah terhubung");
      } else if (res.error === "max_accounts") {
        toast.error("Maksimal akun sudah tercapai");
      } else if (res.error && res.error !== "cancelled") {
        toast.error(`Gagal connect: ${res.error}`);
      }
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Gagal connect";
      toast.error(msg);
    } finally {
      setConnecting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRefreshQuota = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    try {
      await forceRefreshQuota();
      await fetchDrives();
      toast.success("Quota berhasil di-refresh");
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Gagal refresh quota";
      toast.error(msg);
    } finally {
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRemove = (id: string, email: string) => {
    showConfirm(
      "Hapus akun Drive?",
      `File yang tersimpan di ${email} tidak akan bisa diakses.`,
      async () => {
        try {
          await removeDrive(id);
        } catch {
          // error sudah di-toast
        }
      },
    );
  };

  const pct = pool.total > 0 ? Math.round((pool.used / pool.total) * 100) : 0;

  // Text untuk subtitle
  const accountsText = MAX_DRIVES
    ? `${drives.length} dari ${MAX_DRIVES} akun terhubung`
    : `${drives.length} akun terhubung`;

  return (
    <div className="y-anim-fade">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[var(--y-text)]">
            Google Drive Accounts
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            {accountsText} • {formatBytes(pool.used)} dari{" "}
            {formatBytes(pool.total)} terpakai
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={handleRefreshQuota}
            disabled={refreshing}
            title="Refresh quota dari Google"
            className="inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] px-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)] transition-all hover:border-[var(--y-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--y-bg-hover)] hover:text-[var(--y-text)] disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {refreshing ? (
              <Loader2 size={15} className="animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <RefreshCw size={15} />
            )}
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Refresh Quota</span>
          </button>
          {canConnect && (
            <button
              onClick={handleConnect}
              disabled={connecting}
              className="y-gradient-brand y-glow-primary inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:opacity-70"
            >
              {connecting ? (
                <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <Plus size={16} />
              )}
              Hubungkan Drive
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Pool bar */}
      <div className="mb-5 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-4">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            Total Storage Pool • {drives.length} akun
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--y-accent)] tabular-nums">
            {pct}%
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--y-bg)]">
          <motion.div
            className="h-full rounded-full"
            style={{
              background:
                "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-primary), var(--y-accent))",
            }}
            initial={{ width: 0 }}
            animate={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.8, ease: "easeOut" }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Drive grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {drives.map((d, i) => {
          const dpct =
            d.quota_total > 0
              ? Math.min(100, Math.round((d.quota_used / d.quota_total) * 100))
              : 0;
          return (
            <motion.div
              key={d.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: Math.min(i * 0.03, 0.3) }}
              className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-5 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(99,102,241,0.4)] hover:shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.3)]"
            >
              <div
                className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-[3px] opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"
                style={{
                  background:
                    "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-primary), var(--y-accent))",
                }}
              />
              <button
                onClick={() => handleRemove(d.id, d.google_email)}
                title="Hapus"
                className="absolute right-3 top-3 grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-lg text-[var(--y-text-mute)] opacity-0 transition-all hover:bg-[rgba(239,68,68,0.15)] hover:text-[var(--y-danger)] group-hover:opacity-100"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
              </button>

              <div className="mb-3.5 flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-white">
                  <GDriveLogo />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                    {d.google_email}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                    <span>Slot #{d.slot_index}</span>
                    {d.is_active && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[var(--y-success)]">
                        <CheckCircle2 size={11} />
                        aktif
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mb-2 h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--y-bg)]">
                <motion.div
                  className="h-full rounded-full"
                  style={{
                    background:
                      dpct > 80
                        ? "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-warn), var(--y-danger))"
                        : "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-primary), var(--y-accent))",
                  }}
                  initial={{ width: 0 }}
                  animate={{ width: `${dpct}%` }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.8 }}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)] tabular-nums">
                <span>{formatBytes(d.quota_used)} terpakai</span>
                <span>{formatBytes(d.quota_total)} total</span>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}

        {canConnect && (
          <motion.button
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ delay: Math.min(drives.length * 0.03, 0.3) }}
            onClick={handleConnect}
            disabled={connecting}
            className={cn(
              "grid min-h-[180px] place-items-center rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-[var(--y-border)] p-6 text-center transition-all",
              "hover:border-[var(--y-primary)] hover:bg-[rgba(99,102,241,0.05)] disabled:opacity-60",
            )}
          >
            <div>
              <div className="mx-auto mb-3 grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-full border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] text-[var(--y-primary)] transition-transform group-hover:scale-110">
                {connecting ? (
                  <Loader2 size={22} className="animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Plus size={22} />
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                Hubungkan Google Drive
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                {MAX_DRIVES
                  ? `Slot ${drives.length + 1} dari ${MAX_DRIVES}`
                  : `Tambah akun (tidak terbatas)`}
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Info card */}
      <div className="mt-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border-soft)] bg-[rgba(34,211,238,0.05)] p-4">
        <div className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[rgba(34,211,238,0.15)]">
          <Mail size={16} className="text-[var(--y-accent)]" />
        </div>
        <div className="text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
          <b className="text-[var(--y-text)]">Storage pool tak terbatas.</b>{" "}
          Hubungkan sebanyak mungkin akun Google Drive — Yudit Cloud menggabungkan
          semua kapasitasnya menjadi satu cloud virtual. File baru otomatis
          dialokasikan ke drive dengan ruang tersisa paling efisien.
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

    <svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 87.3 78">

        d="m6.6 66.85 3.85 6.65c.8 1.4 1.95 2.5 3.3 3.3l13.75-23.8h-27.5c0 1.55.4 3.1 1.2 4.5z"

===== FILE: src/components/yudit/views/file-row.tsx =====
// src/components/yudit/views/drives-view.tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { Plus, Trash2, Mail, CheckCircle2, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { useYudit } from "@/lib/yudit/store";
import { formatBytes } from "@/lib/yudit/utils";
import { connectGoogleDrive } from "@/lib/api/oauth";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export function DrivesView() {
  const drives = useYudit((s) => s.drives);
  const pool = useYudit((s) => s.pool);
  const removeDrive = useYudit((s) => s.removeDrive);
  const fetchDrives = useYudit((s) => s.fetchDrives);
  const showConfirm = useYudit((s) => s.showConfirm);

  const [connecting, setConnecting] = useState(false);

  const handleConnect = async () => {
    if (drives.length >= 5) return;
    setConnecting(true);
    try {
      const res = await connectGoogleDrive();
      if (res.ok) {
        await fetchDrives();
        toast.success("Akun Google Drive terhubung");
      } else if (res.error && res.error !== "cancelled") {
        toast.error(`Gagal connect: ${res.error}`);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast.error(e?.message || "Gagal connect");
    } finally {
      setConnecting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRemove = (id: string, email: string) => {
    showConfirm(
      "Hapus akun Drive?",
      `File yang tersimpan di ${email} tidak akan bisa diakses.`,
      async () => {
        try {
          await removeDrive(id);
        } catch {
          // error sudah di-toast
        }
      },
    );
  };

  const pct = pool.total > 0 ? Math.round((pool.used / pool.total) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <div className="y-anim-fade">
      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[var(--y-text)]">
            Google Drive Accounts
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            {drives.length} dari 5 akun terhubung • {formatBytes(pool.used)} dari{" "}
            {formatBytes(pool.total)} terpakai
          </p>
        </div>
        {drives.length < 5 && (
          <button
            onClick={handleConnect}
            disabled={connecting}
            className="y-gradient-brand y-glow-primary inline-flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 disabled:opacity-70"
          >
            {connecting ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Plus size={16} />}
            Hubungkan Drive
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-5 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-4">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
            Total Storage Pool
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--y-accent)] tabular-nums">
            {pct}%
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--y-bg)]">
          <motion.div
            className="h-full rounded-full"
            style={{
              background: "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-primary), var(--y-accent))",
            }}
            initial={{ width: 0 }}
            animate={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.8, ease: "easeOut" }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
        {drives.map((d, i) => {
          const dpct =
            d.quota_total > 0
              ? Math.min(100, Math.round((d.quota_used / d.quota_total) * 100))
              : 0;
          return (
            <motion.div
              key={d.id}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
              className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] p-5 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-[rgba(99,102,241,0.4)] hover:shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.3)]"
            >
              <div
                className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-[3px] opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100"
                style={{
                  background: "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-primary), var(--y-accent))",
                }}
              />
              <button
                onClick={() => handleRemove(d.id, d.google_email)}
                title="Hapus"
                className="absolute right-3 top-3 grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-lg text-[var(--y-text-mute)] opacity-0 transition-all hover:bg-[rgba(239,68,68,0.15)] hover:text-[var(--y-danger)] group-hover:opacity-100"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
              </button>

              <div className="mb-3.5 flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-xl bg-white">
                  <GDriveLogo />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="truncate text-[13px] font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                    {d.google_email}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                    <span>Slot #{d.slot_index}</span>
                    {d.is_active && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[var(--y-success)]">
                        <CheckCircle2 size={11} />
                        aktif
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mb-2 h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--y-bg)]">
                <motion.div
                  className="h-full rounded-full"
                  style={{
                    background:
                      dpct > 80
                        ? "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-warn), var(--y-danger))"
                        : "linear-gradient(90deg, var(--y-primary), var(--y-accent))",
                  }}
                  initial={{ width: 0 }}
                  animate={{ width: `${dpct}%` }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.8 }}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-[var(--y-text-dim)] tabular-nums">
                <span>{formatBytes(d.quota_used)} terpakai</span>
                <span>{formatBytes(d.quota_total)} total</span>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          );
        })}

        {drives.length < 5 && (
          <motion.button
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ delay: drives.length * 0.05 }}
            onClick={handleConnect}
            disabled={connecting}
            className={cn(
              "grid min-h-[180px] place-items-center rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-[var(--y-border)] p-6 text-center transition-all",
              "hover:border-[var(--y-primary)] hover:bg-[rgba(99,102,241,0.05)] disabled:opacity-60",
            )}
          >
            <div>
              <div className="mx-auto mb-3 grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-full border border-[var(--y-border)] bg-[var(--y-bg-elev)] text-[var(--y-primary)] transition-transform group-hover:scale-110">
                {connecting ? (
                  <Loader2 size={22} className="animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Plus size={22} />
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--y-text)]">
                Hubungkan Google Drive
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--y-text-mute)]">
                Slot {drives.length + 1} dari 5
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--y-border-soft)] bg-[rgba(34,211,238,0.05)] p-4">
        <div className="grid h-9 w-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-[rgba(34,211,238,0.15)]">
          <Mail size={16} className="text-[var(--y-accent)]" />
        </div>
        <div className="text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-[var(--y-text-dim)]">
          <b className="text-[var(--y-text)]">Storage pool otomatis.</b> Yudit Cloud
          menggabungkan kapasitas semua akun Google Drive yang terhubung menjadi satu
          cloud virtual. File baru otomatis dialokasikan ke drive dengan ruang tersisa
          paling besar.
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/hooks/use-mobile.ts =====
import * as React from "react"

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    }
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange)
  }, [])

  return !!isMobile
}



===== FILE: src/hooks/use-toast.ts =====
"use client"

// Inspired by react-hot-toast library
import * as React from "react"

import type {
  ToastActionElement,
  ToastProps,
} from "@/components/ui/toast"

const TOAST_LIMIT = 1
const TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000

type ToasterToast = ToastProps & {
  id: string
  title?: React.ReactNode
  description?: React.ReactNode
  action?: ToastActionElement
}

const actionTypes = {
  ADD_TOAST: "ADD_TOAST",
  UPDATE_TOAST: "UPDATE_TOAST",
  DISMISS_TOAST: "DISMISS_TOAST",
  REMOVE_TOAST: "REMOVE_TOAST",
} as const

let count = 0

function genId() {
  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  return count.toString()
}

type ActionType = typeof actionTypes

type Action =
  | {
    type: ActionType["ADD_TOAST"]
    toast: ToasterToast
  }
  | {
    type: ActionType["UPDATE_TOAST"]
    toast: Partial<ToasterToast>
  }
  | {
    type: ActionType["DISMISS_TOAST"]
    toastId?: ToasterToast["id"]
  }
  | {
    type: ActionType["REMOVE_TOAST"]
    toastId?: ToasterToast["id"]
  }

interface State {
  toasts: ToasterToast[]
}

const toastTimeouts = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>()

const addToRemoveQueue = (toastId: string) => {
  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {
    return
  }

  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId)
    dispatch({
      type: "REMOVE_TOAST",
      toastId: toastId,
    })
  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY)

  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout)
}

export const reducer = (state: State, action: Action): State => {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "ADD_TOAST":
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),
      }

    case "UPDATE_TOAST":
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t
        ),
      }

    case "DISMISS_TOAST": {
      const { toastId } = action

      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,
      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity
      if (toastId) {
        addToRemoveQueue(toastId)
      } else {
        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {
          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id)
        })
      }

      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined
            ? {
              ...t,
              open: false,
            }
            : t
        ),
      }
    }
    case "REMOVE_TOAST":
      if (action.toastId === undefined) {
        return {
          ...state,
          toasts: [],
        }
      }
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),
      }
  }
}

const listeners: Array<(state: State) => void> = []

let memoryState: State = { toasts: [] }

function dispatch(action: Action) {
  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action)
  listeners.forEach((listener) => {
    listener(memoryState)
  })
}

type Toast = Omit<ToasterToast, "id">

function toast({ ...props }: Toast) {
  const id = genId()

  const update = (props: ToasterToast) =>
    dispatch({
      type: "UPDATE_TOAST",
      toast: { ...props, id },
    })
  const dismiss = () => dispatch({ type: "DISMISS_TOAST", toastId: id })

  dispatch({
    type: "ADD_TOAST",
    toast: {
      ...props,
      id,
      open: true,
      onOpenChange: (open) => {
        if (!open) dismiss()
      },
    },
  })

  return {
    id: id,
    dismiss,
    update,
  }
}

function useToast() {
  const [state, setState] = React.useState<State>(memoryState)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    listeners.push(setState)
    return () => {
      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState)
      if (index > -1) {
        listeners.splice(index, 1)
      }
    }
  }, [state])

  return {
    ...state,
    toast,
    dismiss: (toastId?: string) => dispatch({ type: "DISMISS_TOAST", toastId }),
  }
}

export { useToast, toast }

===== FILE: src/lib/yudit/utils.ts =====
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  FileText,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Film,
  Music,
  Archive,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Presentation,
  Code2,
  File,
  FileType2,
} from "lucide-react";

export function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (!bytes || bytes === 0) return "0 B";
  const k = 1024;
  const sizes = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + " " + sizes[i];
}

export function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return d.toLocaleDateString("id-ID", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

export function formatDateTime(iso: string): string {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return d.toLocaleString("id-ID", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
}

export const MONTHS_ID = [
  "Januari",
  "Februari",
  "Maret",
  "April",
  "Mei",
  "Juni",
  "Juli",
  "Agustus",
  "September",
  "Oktober",
  "November",
  "Desember",
];

export const MONTHS_SHORT = [
  "Jan",
  "Feb",
  "Mar",
  "Apr",
  "Mei",
  "Jun",
  "Jul",
  "Ags",
  "Sep",
  "Okt",
  "Nov",
  "Des",
];

export function extOf(name: string): string {
  return (name.split(".").pop() || "").toLowerCase();
}

export interface FileIconInfo {
  Icon: LucideIcon;
  color: string;
  bg: string;
}

export function fileIconInfo(
  mime: string,
  name = "",
): FileIconInfo {
  const ext = extOf(name);
  if (
    mime.startsWith("image/") ||
    ["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "gif", "webp", "svg", "bmp", "heic"].includes(ext)
  ) {
    return {
      Icon: ImageIcon,
      color: "#22d3ee",
      bg: "rgba(34, 211, 238, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  if (
    mime.startsWith("video/") ||
    ["mp4", "mkv", "mov", "avi", "webm"].includes(ext)
  ) {
    return {
      Icon: Film,
      color: "#f87171",
      bg: "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  if (
    mime.startsWith("audio/") ||
    ["mp3", "wav", "flac", "m4a", "ogg"].includes(ext)
  ) {
    return {
      Icon: Music,
      color: "#e879f9",
      bg: "rgba(217, 70, 239, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  if (["zip", "rar", "7z", "tar", "gz"].includes(ext)) {
    return {
      Icon: Archive,
      color: "#d6d3d1",
      bg: "rgba(120, 113, 108, 0.18)",
    };
  }
  if (ext === "pdf") {
    return {
      Icon: FileType2,
      color: "#f87171",
      bg: "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  if (["doc", "docx", "odt", "rtf"].includes(ext)) {
    return {
      Icon: FileText,
      color: "#60a5fa",
      bg: "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  if (["xls", "xlsx", "csv", "ods"].includes(ext)) {
    return {
      Icon: FileSpreadsheet,
      color: "#34d399",
      bg: "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  if (["ppt", "pptx", "odp"].includes(ext)) {
    return {
      Icon: Presentation,
      color: "#fbbf24",
      bg: "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  if (
    ["js", "ts", "py", "html", "css", "json", "sh", "java", "go", "sql", "xml", "md", "txt"].includes(
      ext,
    )
  ) {
    return {
      Icon: Code2,
      color: "#a5b4fc",
      bg: "rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.12)",
    };
  }
  return {
    Icon: File,
    color: "#94a3b8",
    bg: "rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.12)",
  };
}
