export function getCategoryMeta(
  cat: string | null,
  customCats: { name: string; icon: string }[] = [],
): CategoryMeta {
  if (!cat)
    return {
      icon: "📄",
      cls: "cat-lainnya",
      color: "#94a3b8",
      bg: "rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.14)",
      border: "rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.3)",
    };
  if (CATEGORY_META[cat]) return CATEGORY_META[cat];
  const custom = customCats.find((c) => c.name === cat);
  return {
    icon: custom?.icon || "📄",
    cls: "cat-custom",
    color: "#a5b4fc",
    bg: "rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.14)",
    border: "rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3)",
  };
}
// src/lib/yudit/utils.ts — TAMBAHKAN di bagian bawah (setelah getCategoryMeta)

export function getCategoryMetaSafe(
  cat: string | null,
  customCats: { name: string; icon: string; color?: string }[] = [],
): CategoryMeta {
  if (!cat) {
    return {
      icon: "📄",
      cls: "cat-lainnya",
      color: "#94a3b8",
      bg: "rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.14)",
      border: "rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.3)",
    };
  }
  if (CATEGORY_META[cat]) return CATEGORY_META[cat];
  const custom = customCats.find((c) => c.name === cat);
  return {
    icon: custom?.icon || "📄",
    cls: "cat-custom",
    color: custom?.color || "#a5b4fc",
    bg: "rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.14)",
    border: "rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.3)",
  };
}

export function detectCategory(
  name: string,
  mime: string,
): string {
  const ext = extOf(name);
  const lower = name.toLowerCase();

  const RULES: { cat: string; test: () => boolean }[] = [
    { cat: "Surat Masuk", test: () => /surat\s*masuk|incoming|masuk/i.test(lower) },
    { cat: "Surat Keluar", test: () => /surat\s*keluar|outgoing|keluar/i.test(lower) },
    { cat: "BA (Berita Acara)", test: () => /berita\s*acara|\bba\b|\bnotula/i.test(lower) },
    {
      cat: "Surat Keputusan Pimpinan",
      test: () => /keputusan\s*pimpinan|sk\s*pimpinan/i.test(lower),
    },
    { cat: "Surat Keputusan DPRD", test: () => /keputusan\s*dprd|sk\s*dprd/i.test(lower) },
    {
      cat: "Surat Persetujuan Bersama",
      test: () => /persetujuan\s*bersama/i.test(lower),
    },
    {
      cat: "SPJ (Surat Pertanggungjawaban)",
      test: () => /spj|pertanggungjawab/i.test(lower),
    },
    { cat: "Notulen Banmus", test: () => /notulen\s*banmus|banmus/i.test(lower) },
    {
      cat: "Notulen Bapemperda",
      test: () => /notulen\s*bapem|bapemperda/i.test(lower),
    },
    { cat: "Notulen Pansus", test: () => /notulen\s*pansus|pansus/i.test(lower) },
  ];

  for (const r of RULES) {
    if (r.test()) return r.cat;
  }

  if (mime.startsWith("image/") || ["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "gif", "webp", "svg"].includes(ext))
    return "Gambar";
  if (mime.startsWith("video/") || ["mp4", "mov", "mkv", "webm"].includes(ext))
    return "Video";
  if (mime.startsWith("audio/") || ["mp3", "wav", "flac", "m4a"].includes(ext))
    return "Audio";
  if (["zip", "rar", "7z", "tar", "gz"].includes(ext)) return "Arsip";
  if (["xls", "xlsx", "csv", "ods"].includes(ext)) return "Spreadsheet";
  if (["ppt", "pptx", "odp"].includes(ext)) return "Presentasi";
  if (["js", "ts", "py", "html", "css", "json", "sh", "sql", "xml"].includes(ext))
    return "Kode";
  if (["pdf", "doc", "docx", "odt", "rtf", "txt", "md"].includes(ext)) return "Dokumen";
  return "Lainnya";
}

export function genId(prefix = "id"): string {
  return prefix + "_" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10) + Date.now().toString(36);
}

export function genToken(): string {
  return (
    Math.random().toString(36).slice(2) +
    Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)
  );
}

// src/lib/yudit/types.ts
export type ViewKey = "files" | "monthly" | "stats" | "drives";

export interface User {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
}

/** Mirror dari ApiFile backend */
export interface FileItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  mime_type: string | null;
  size: number;
  category: string | null;
  category_confidence?: number;
  folder_id: string | null;
  folder_name: string | null;
  monthly_folder_id?: string | null;
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day?: number;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at?: string;
  drive_account_id: string;
  file_type?: string | null;
  is_public: boolean;
  share_token: string | null;
  share_expires_at?: string | null;
  share_views: number;
}

export interface DriveAccount {
  id: string;
  google_email: string;
  display_name?: string | null;
  slot_index: number;
  is_active: boolean;
  quota_total: number;
  quota_used: number;
}

export interface Category {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  icon: string;
  color: string;
  keywords: string[];
  is_builtin: boolean;
}

export interface Filters {
  year?: number;
  month?: number;
  day?: number;
  date_from?: string;
  date_to?: string;
  file_type?: string;
  sort_by?: "created_at" | "name" | "size" | "year" | "month";
  sort_order?: "asc" | "desc";
}

export interface PoolInfo {
  total: number;
  used: number;
  free: number;
  accounts: number;
}

export interface Stats {
  total_files: number;
  total_size: number;
  by_year: Record<string, number>;
  by_month: Record<string, number>;
  by_category: Record<string, number>;
  by_type: Record<string, number>;
  by_day?: Record<string, number>;
}

export interface ShareConfig {
  expires_hours: number | null;
  password: string | null;
}

export function buildSeedFiles(): FileItem[] {
  const MONTHS_ID = [
    "Januari",
    "Februari",
    "Maret",
    "April",
    "Mei",
    "Juni",
    "Juli",
    "Agustus",
    "September",
    "Oktober",
    "November",
    "Desember",
  ];

  return SEED.map((s, i) => {
    const created = new Date(s.year, s.month - 1, s.day, 10 + (i % 6), (i * 7) % 60);
    const category = detectCategory(s.name, s.mime);
    return {
      id: "file_" + (i + 1),
      name: s.name,
      mime_type: s.mime,
      size: s.size,
      category,
      folder_id: null,
      folder_name: `${MONTHS_ID[s.month - 1]} ${s.year}`,
      year: s.year,
      month: s.month,
      created_at: created.toISOString(),
      drive_id: s.drive_id,
      is_public: s.is_public || false,
      share_token: s.is_public ? "sh_" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 14) : null,
      share_views: s.share_views || 0,
    } satisfies FileItem;
  });
}

export function buildStats(files: FileItem[]): Stats {
  const by_year: Record<string, number> = {};
  const by_month: Record<string, number> = {};
  const by_category: Record<string, number> = {};
  const by_type: Record<string, number> = {};

  let total_size = 0;
  for (const f of files) {
    total_size += f.size;
    by_year[String(f.year)] = (by_year[String(f.year)] || 0) + 1;
    const mk = `${f.year}-${String(f.month).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    by_month[mk] = (by_month[mk] || 0) + 1;
    if (f.category) by_category[f.category] = (by_category[f.category] || 0) + 1;
    const ext = extOf(f.name);
    by_type[ext || "unknown"] = (by_type[ext || "unknown"] || 0) + 1;
  }

  return {
    total_files: files.length,
    total_size,
    by_year,
    by_month,
    by_category,
    by_type,
  };
}



===== FILE: src/lib/yudit/store.ts =====
// src/lib/yudit/store.ts
"use client";

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";

import type { DriveAccount, FileItem, Filters, PoolInfo, User, ViewKey } from "./types";
import * as authApi from "@/lib/api/auth";
import * as filesApi from "@/lib/api/files";
import * as drivesApi from "@/lib/api/drives";
import * as catApi from "@/lib/api/categories";
import * as shareApi from "@/lib/api/share";
import {
  clearToken,
  getStoredUser,
  getToken,
  setStoredUser,
  setToken,
  ApiError,
} from "@/lib/api/client";

// =====================================================
// TOAST BRIDGE
// =====================================================
type ToastFn = (msg: string, type?: "success" | "error" | "info") => void;
let toastFn: ToastFn | null = null;
export function registerToast(fn: ToastFn) {
  toastFn = fn;
}
function toast(msg: string, type: "success" | "error" | "info" = "info") {
  toastFn?.(msg, type);
}

// =====================================================
// STATE INTERFACE
// =====================================================
interface YuditState {
  // auth
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  user: User | null;
  authLoading: boolean;

  // views
  currentView: ViewKey;
  sidebarOpen: boolean;

  // data
  drives: DriveAccount[];
  pool: PoolInfo;
  files: FileItem[];
  customCategories: catApi.ApiCategory[];
  defaultCategories: catApi.ApiCategory[];

  // loading flags
  filesLoading: boolean;
  drivesLoading: boolean;
  quotaRefreshing: boolean;   // ← BARU

  // files view state
  currentCategory: string;
  searchQuery: string;
  monthlySearchQuery: string;
  filters: Filters;
  filterPanelOpen: boolean;
  expandedMonthly: Record<string, boolean>;

  // modals
  previewFileId: string | null;
  shareFileId: string | null;
  categoryManagerOpen: boolean;
  confirmDialog: {
    open: boolean;
    title: string;
    text: string;
    onConfirm: (() => void) | null;
  };
  progress: {
    open: boolean;
    label: string;
    sub: string;
    pct: number | null;
  };

  // ============ ACTIONS ============
  // auth
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (email: string, username: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => void;
  bootstrapAuth: () => void;

  // views
  setView: (v: ViewKey) => void;
  setSidebarOpen: (open: boolean) => void;

  // data fetch
  fetchFiles: (params?: filesApi.FileListParams) => Promise<void>;
  fetchDrives: () => Promise<void>;
  fetchCategories: () => Promise<void>;

  // drives
  refreshPool: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshQuota: () => Promise<void>;   // ← BARU: force refresh quota
  removeDrive: (id: string) => Promise<void>;

  // files
  uploadFiles: (files: File[]) => Promise<void>;
  deleteFile: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  updateFileCategory: (id: string, category: string) => Promise<void>;

  // filters
  setCategory: (cat: string) => void;
  setSearch: (q: string) => void;
  setMonthlySearch: (q: string) => void;
  setFilters: (f: Filters) => void;
  resetFilters: () => void;
  setFilterPanelOpen: (open: boolean) => void;

  // monthly
  toggleMonthly: (key: string) => void;
  collapseAllMonthly: () => void;
  expandAllMonthly: () => void;

  // modals
  openPreview: (id: string) => void;
  closePreview: () => void;
  openShare: (id: string) => void;
  closeShare: () => void;
  createShare: (cfg: {
    expires_hours: number | null;
    password: string | null;
  }) => Promise<void>;
  revokeShare: (id: string) => Promise<void>;

  // categories
  setCategoryManagerOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  addCustomCategory: (
    name: string,
    icon: string,
    keywords: string[],
  ) => Promise<void>;
  removeCustomCategory: (id: string) => Promise<void>;

  // ui
  showConfirm: (title: string, text: string, onConfirm: () => void) => void;
  closeConfirm: () => void;
  showProgress: (label: string, sub: string, pct?: number | null) => void;
  hideProgress: () => void;
}

const EMPTY_POOL: PoolInfo = { total: 0, used: 0, free: 0, accounts: 0 };

// =====================================================
// STORE
// =====================================================
export const useYudit = create<YuditState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      // ---- initial state ----
      isAuthenticated: false,
      user: null,
      authLoading: false,

      currentView: "files",
      sidebarOpen: false,

      drives: [],
      pool: EMPTY_POOL,
      files: [],
      customCategories: [],
      defaultCategories: [],

      filesLoading: false,
      drivesLoading: false,
      quotaRefreshing: false,

      currentCategory: "all",
      searchQuery: "",
      monthlySearchQuery: "",
      filters: {},
      filterPanelOpen: false,
      expandedMonthly: {},

      previewFileId: null,
      shareFileId: null,
      categoryManagerOpen: false,
      confirmDialog: { open: false, title: "", text: "", onConfirm: null },
      progress: { open: false, label: "", sub: "", pct: null },

      // =====================================================
      // AUTH
      // =====================================================
      bootstrapAuth: () => {
        const token = getToken();
        const user = getStoredUser<User>();
        if (token && user) {
          set({ isAuthenticated: true, user });
        } else {
          set({ isAuthenticated: false, user: null });
        }
      },

      login: async (email, password) => {
        set({ authLoading: true });
        try {
          const res = await authApi.login({ email, password });
          setToken(res.access_token);
          setStoredUser(res.user);
          set({ isAuthenticated: true, user: res.user, authLoading: false });
          toast(`Selamat datang, ${res.user.username}!`, "success");
        } catch (e) {
          set({ authLoading: false });
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Login gagal";
          toast(msg, "error");
          throw e;
        }
      },

      register: async (email, username, password) => {
        set({ authLoading: true });
        try {
          const res = await authApi.register({ email, username, password });
          setToken(res.access_token);
          setStoredUser(res.user);
          set({ isAuthenticated: true, user: res.user, authLoading: false });
          toast(
            `Akun berhasil dibuat. Selamat datang, ${res.user.username}!`,
            "success",
          );
        } catch (e) {
          set({ authLoading: false });
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Registrasi gagal";
          toast(msg, "error");
          throw e;
        }
      },

      logout: () => {
        clearToken();
        set({
          isAuthenticated: false,
          user: null,
          currentView: "files",
          previewFileId: null,
          shareFileId: null,
          files: [],
          drives: [],
          pool: EMPTY_POOL,
          customCategories: [],
          defaultCategories: [],
        });
        toast("Logout berhasil", "info");
      },

      // =====================================================
      // VIEWS
      // =====================================================
      setView: (v) => {
        set({ currentView: v, sidebarOpen: false });
        if (v === "files") {
          const { filters, currentCategory, searchQuery } = get();
          get().fetchFiles({
            category: currentCategory !== "all" ? currentCategory : undefined,
            search: searchQuery || undefined,
            ...filters,
          });
        }
        if (v === "drives") get().fetchDrives();
        if (v === "monthly") get().fetchFiles();
        if (v === "stats") get().fetchFiles();
      },

      setSidebarOpen: (open) => set({ sidebarOpen: open }),

      // =====================================================
      // DATA FETCH
      // =====================================================
      fetchFiles: async (params) => {
        set({ filesLoading: true });
        try {
          const files = await filesApi.listFiles(params || {});
          set({ files: files as FileItem[], filesLoading: false });
        } catch (e) {
          set({ filesLoading: false });
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal memuat file";
          toast(msg, "error");
        }
      },

      fetchDrives: async () => {
        set({ drivesLoading: true });
        try {
          const [drives, pool] = await Promise.all([
            drivesApi.listDrives(),
            drivesApi.getPoolSummary().catch(() => EMPTY_POOL),
          ]);
          set({
            drives: drives as DriveAccount[],
            pool: pool as PoolInfo,
            drivesLoading: false,
          });
        } catch (e) {
          set({ drivesLoading: false });
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal memuat drive";
          toast(msg, "error");
        }
      },

      fetchCategories: async () => {
        try {
          const res = await catApi.listCategories();
          set({
            defaultCategories: res.default.map((c) => ({
              ...c,
              is_builtin: true,
            })),
            customCategories: res.custom.map((c) => ({
              ...c,
              is_builtin: false,
              keywords: c.keywords || [],
            })),
          });
        } catch (e) {
          const msg =
            e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal memuat kategori";
          toast(msg, "error");
        }
      },

      refreshPool: async () => {
        try {
          const pool = await drivesApi.getPoolSummary();
          set({ pool: pool as PoolInfo });
        } catch {
          // silent
        }
      },

      // =====================================================
      // REFRESH QUOTA (BARU) — force refresh dari Google
      // =====================================================
      refreshQuota: async () => {
        if (get().quotaRefreshing) return; // prevent double trigger
        set({ quotaRefreshing: true });
        try {
          await drivesApi.forceRefreshQuota();
          // Refresh data drives + pool setelah refresh quota
          await get().fetchDrives();
          toast("Quota berhasil di-refresh", "success");
        } catch (e) {
          const msg =
            e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal refresh quota";
          toast(msg, "error");
        } finally {
          set({ quotaRefreshing: false });
        }
      },

      removeDrive: async (id) => {
        try {
          await drivesApi.disconnectDrive(id);
          toast("Akun Drive dihapus", "success");
          await get().fetchDrives();
        } catch (e) {
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal hapus drive";
          toast(msg, "error");
          throw e;
        }
      },

      // =====================================================
      // FILES
      // =====================================================
      uploadFiles: async (fileList) => {
        if (fileList.length === 0) return;

        const total = fileList.length;
        let done = 0;

        set({
          progress: {
            open: true,
            label: `Mengunggah 0/${total}`,
            sub: "Menyiapkan…",
            pct: 0,
          },
        });

        for (const file of fileList) {
          try {
            await filesApi.uploadFile(file, undefined, (pct) => {
              const overall = Math.round(
                ((done + pct / 100) / total) * 100,
              );
              set({
                progress: {
                  open: true,
                  label: `Mengunggah ${done + 1}/${total}`,
                  sub: file.name,
                  pct: overall,
                },
              });
            });
            done++;
            toast(`"${file.name}" berhasil diunggah`, "success");
          } catch (e) {
            const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Upload gagal";
            toast(`Gagal upload "${file.name}": ${msg}`, "error");
          }
        }

        set({ progress: { open: false, label: "", sub: "", pct: null } });

        // refresh list + pool
        const { filters, currentCategory, searchQuery } = get();
        await get().fetchFiles({
          category: currentCategory !== "all" ? currentCategory : undefined,
          search: searchQuery || undefined,
          ...filters,
        });
        get().refreshPool();
      },

      deleteFile: async (id) => {
        try {
          await filesApi.deleteFile(id);
          set((s) => ({ files: s.files.filter((f) => f.id !== id) }));
          toast("File dihapus", "success");
          get().refreshPool();
        } catch (e) {
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal hapus file";
          toast(msg, "error");
        }
      },

      updateFileCategory: async (id, category) => {
        try {
          const res = await filesApi.updateCategory(id, category);
          set((s) => ({
            files: s.files.map((f) =>
              f.id === id ? { ...f, ...res.file } : f,
            ),
          }));
          toast("Kategori diperbarui", "success");
        } catch (e) {
          const msg =
            e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal update kategori";
          toast(msg, "error");
        }
      },

      // =====================================================
      // FILTERS
      // =====================================================
      setCategory: (cat) => {
        set({ currentCategory: cat });
        const { filters, searchQuery } = get();
        get().fetchFiles({
          category: cat !== "all" ? cat : undefined,
          search: searchQuery || undefined,
          ...filters,
        });
      },

      setSearch: (q) => {
        set({ searchQuery: q });
        const { filters, currentCategory } = get();
        get().fetchFiles({
          category: currentCategory !== "all" ? currentCategory : undefined,
          search: q || undefined,
          ...filters,
        });
      },

      setMonthlySearch: (q) => set({ monthlySearchQuery: q }),

      setFilters: (f) => {
        set({ filters: f });
        const { currentCategory, searchQuery } = get();
        get().fetchFiles({
          category: currentCategory !== "all" ? currentCategory : undefined,
          search: searchQuery || undefined,
          ...f,
        });
      },

      resetFilters: () => {
        set({ filters: {} });
        const { currentCategory, searchQuery } = get();
        get().fetchFiles({
          category: currentCategory !== "all" ? currentCategory : undefined,
          search: searchQuery || undefined,
        });
      },

      setFilterPanelOpen: (open) => set({ filterPanelOpen: open }),

      // =====================================================
      // MONTHLY
      // =====================================================
      toggleMonthly: (key) =>
        set((s) => ({
          expandedMonthly: {
            ...s.expandedMonthly,
            [key]: !s.expandedMonthly[key],
          },
        })),

      collapseAllMonthly: () => set({ expandedMonthly: {} }),

      expandAllMonthly: () => {
        const grouped = groupByFolder(get().files);
        const out: Record<string, boolean> = {};
        Object.keys(grouped).forEach((k) => (out[k] = true));
        set({ expandedMonthly: out });
      },

      // =====================================================
      // MODALS
      // =====================================================
      openPreview: (id) => set({ previewFileId: id }),
      closePreview: () => set({ previewFileId: null }),
      openShare: (id) => set({ shareFileId: id }),
      closeShare: () => set({ shareFileId: null }),

      createShare: async (cfg) => {
        const id = get().shareFileId;
        if (!id) return;
        try {
          const res = await shareApi.createShare({
            file_id: id,
            expires_hours: cfg.expires_hours,
            password: cfg.password,
          });
          set((s) => ({
            files: s.files.map((f) =>
              f.id === id
                ? {
                    ...f,
                    is_public: true,
                    share_token: res.token,
                    share_expires_at: res.expires_at,
                  }
                : f,
            ),
          }));
          toast("Link share dibuat", "success");
        } catch (e) {
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal buat share";
          toast(msg, "error");
          throw e;
        }
      },

      revokeShare: async (id) => {
        try {
          await shareApi.revokeShare(id);
          set((s) => ({
            files: s.files.map((f) =>
              f.id === id
                ? { ...f, is_public: false, share_token: null, share_views: 0 }
                : f,
            ),
          }));
          toast("Share dicabut", "success");
        } catch (e) {
          const msg = e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal cabut share";
          toast(msg, "error");
        }
      },

      // =====================================================
      // CATEGORIES
      // =====================================================
      setCategoryManagerOpen: (open) => set({ categoryManagerOpen: open }),

      addCustomCategory: async (name, icon, keywords) => {
        try {
          await catApi.createCategory({ name, icon, keywords });
          toast(`Kategori "${name}" ditambahkan`, "success");
          await get().fetchCategories();
        } catch (e) {
          const msg =
            e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal tambah kategori";
          toast(msg, "error");
          throw e;
        }
      },

      removeCustomCategory: async (id) => {
        try {
          await catApi.deleteCategory(id);
          toast("Kategori dihapus", "success");
          await get().fetchCategories();
        } catch (e) {
          const msg =
            e instanceof ApiError ? e.detail : "Gagal hapus kategori";
          toast(msg, "error");
        }
      },

      // =====================================================
      // UI
      // =====================================================
      showConfirm: (title, text, onConfirm) =>
        set({ confirmDialog: { open: true, title, text, onConfirm } }),

      closeConfirm: () =>
        set((s) => ({
          confirmDialog: {
            ...s.confirmDialog,
            open: false,
            onConfirm: null,
          },
        })),

      showProgress: (label, sub, pct = null) =>
        set({ progress: { open: true, label, sub, pct } }),

      hideProgress: () =>
        set((s) => ({ progress: { ...s.progress, open: false } })),
    }),
    {
      name: "yudit-cloud-store",
      partialize: (s) => ({
        currentView: s.currentView,
        filters: s.filters,
        expandedMonthly: s.expandedMonthly,
      }),
    },
  ),
);

// =====================================================
// HELPERS
// =====================================================
export function groupByFolder(
  files: FileItem[],
): Record<string, FileItem[]> {
  const grouped: Record<string, FileItem[]> = {};
  for (const f of files) {
    const key = f.folder_name || "Tanpa Folder";
    if (!grouped[key]) grouped[key] = [];
    grouped[key].push(f);
  }
  return grouped;
}

export function categoryCounts(
  files: FileItem[],
): Record<string, number> {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const f of files) {
    if (f.category) out[f.category] = (out[f.category] || 0) + 1;
  }
  return out;
}

export function availableYears(files: FileItem[]): string[] {
  const set = new Set<string>();
  files.forEach((f) => set.add(String(f.year)));
  return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => Number(b) - Number(a));
}

// =====================================================
// GLOBAL EVENT LISTENER — unauthorized → logout
// =====================================================
if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  window.addEventListener("yudit:unauthorized", () => {
    useYudit.getState().logout();
  });
}


===== FILE: src/lib/api/oauth.ts =====
// src/lib/api/oauth.ts
import { api } from "./client";

// =====================================================
// GET AUTH URL
// =====================================================
export async function getGoogleConnectUrl(): Promise<string> {
  const { data } = await api.get<{ auth_url: string }>("/oauth/google/connect");
  return data.auth_url;
}

// =====================================================
// RESULT TYPE
// =====================================================
export interface OAuthResult {
  ok: boolean;
  error?: string;
  /** Error code yang mungkin: 
   *  - "cancelled"         → user tutup popup manual
   *  - "popup_blocked"     → popup diblokir browser
   *  - "max_accounts"      → batas akun tercapai (kalau di-set)
   *  - "duplicate_account" → email Google sudah pernah connect
   *  - "no_refresh_token"  → Google tidak kirim refresh token
   *  - "invalid_state"     → state JWT tidak valid
   *  - "network_error"     → gagal fetch auth_url
   *  - <lainnya>           → pesan error dari server
   */
}

// =====================================================
// CONNECT GOOGLE DRIVE (via popup OAuth)
// =====================================================
/**
 * Buka popup Google OAuth, tunggu sampai callback redirect.
 *
 * Backend akan redirect ke salah satu URL:
 *   - `{FRONTEND_URL}/drives?success=connected`
 *   - `{FRONTEND_URL}/drives?error=max_accounts`
 *   - `{FRONTEND_URL}/drives?error=duplicate_account`
 *   - `{FRONTEND_URL}/drives?error=no_refresh_token`
 *
 * Frontend (halaman `/drives` atau oauth-callback) akan
 * mengirim `postMessage` dengan payload:
 *   `{ type: "yudit:oauth", success: boolean, error?: string }`
 *
 * Function ini listen `message` event sampai menerima payload,
 * atau sampai popup ditutup manual.
 */
export function connectGoogleDrive(): Promise<OAuthResult> {
  return new Promise(async (resolve) => {
    let resolved = false;
    let pollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

    const cleanup = (popup?: Window | null) => {
      if (pollTimer) {
        clearInterval(pollTimer);
        pollTimer = null;
      }
      window.removeEventListener("message", handler);
      try {
        popup?.close();
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
    };

    const finish = (result: OAuthResult, popup?: Window | null) => {
      if (resolved) return;
      resolved = true;
      cleanup(popup);
      resolve(result);
    };

    // ---- Message handler (dari callback page) ----
    const handler = (e: MessageEvent) => {
      if (!e.data || e.data.type !== "yudit:oauth") return;
      finish(
        {
          ok: !!e.data.success,
          error: e.data.error || undefined,
        },
        currentPopup,
      );
    };

    let currentPopup: Window | null = null;

    try {
      // ---- 1. Ambil auth_url dari backend ----
      const url = await getGoogleConnectUrl();
      if (!url) {
        finish({ ok: false, error: "auth_url tidak ditemukan" });
        return;
      }

      // ---- 2. Buka popup ----
      const w = 520;
      const h = 640;
      const dualScreenLeft = window.screenLeft ?? window.screenX ?? 0;
      const dualScreenTop = window.screenTop ?? window.screenY ?? 0;
      const width = window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth || screen.width;
      const height = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || screen.height;
      const left = width / 2 - w / 2 + dualScreenLeft;
      const top = height / 2 - h / 2 + dualScreenTop;

      const popup = window.open(
        url,
        "google-oauth-yudit",
        `width=${w},height=${h},left=${left},top=${top},resizable=yes,scrollbars=yes`,
      );

      currentPopup = popup;

      if (!popup) {
        finish({ ok: false, error: "popup_blocked" });
        return;
      }

      // ---- 3. Listen message ----
      window.addEventListener("message", handler);

      // ---- 4. Polling: cek apakah popup ditutup manual ----
      pollTimer = setInterval(() => {
        try {
          if (!popup || popup.closed) {
            // Popup ditutup. Beri sedikit delay untuk handle race condition
            // (kalau postMessage datang terlambat).
            setTimeout(() => {
              finish({ ok: false, error: "cancelled" }, popup);
            }, 300);
          }
        } catch {
          // Cross-origin error (biasanya karena popup masih di halaman Google)
          // → skip cek, cek di iterasi berikutnya
        }
      }, 800);
    } catch (e: unknown) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Gagal membuka OAuth";
      finish({ ok: false, error: msg });
    }
  });
}

  has_password: boolean;
  views: number;
}

  payload: ShareCreatePayload,
): Promise<ShareCreateResponse> {
  const { data } = await api.post<ShareCreateResponse>("/share/create", payload);
  return data;
}

export async function revokeShare(fileId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api.delete(`/share/${fileId}`);
}

/**
 * URL publik untuk share link — dipakai user untuk share ke orang lain.
 * Mengarah ke halaman frontend /share/[token] (BUKAN API).
 */
export function getShareUrl(token: string): string {
  const base =
    typeof window !== "undefined"
      ? window.location.origin
      : process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || "";
  return `${base}/share/${token}`;
}

/**
 * URL API untuk download via share token.
 */
export function getShareDownloadUrl(token: string, password?: string): string {
  const q = password ? `?password=${encodeURIComponent(password)}` : "";
  return getApiUrl(`/share/${token}/download${q}`);
}

/**
 * URL API untuk preview via share token.
 */
export function getSharePreviewUrl(token: string, password?: string): string {
  const q = password ? `?password=${encodeURIComponent(password)}` : "";
  return getApiUrl(`/share/${token}/preview${q}`);
}

export async function getShareInfo(token: string): Promise<ShareInfo> {
  const { data } = await api.get<ShareInfo>(`/share/${token}/info`);
  return data;
}


===== FILE: src/lib/api/client.ts =====
// src/lib/api/client.ts
import axios, { AxiosError, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from "axios";

const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://localhost:8000";

export const TOKEN_KEY = "yudit_token";
export const USER_KEY = "yudit_user";

export const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_URL,
  timeout: 120_000, // 2 menit — upload file besar
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
});

// =====================================================
// TOKEN STORAGE
// =====================================================
export function getToken(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
}

export function setToken(token: string): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
}

export function clearToken(): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
  localStorage.removeItem(USER_KEY);
}

export function getStoredUser<T = unknown>(): T | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(USER_KEY);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function setStoredUser(user: unknown): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  localStorage.setItem(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
}

// =====================================================
// REQUEST INTERCEPTOR
// =====================================================
api.interceptors.request.use((config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
  const token = getToken();
  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }
  return config;
});

// =====================================================
// RESPONSE INTERCEPTOR
// =====================================================
export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  detail: string;
  constructor(status: number, detail: string) {
    super(detail);
    this.status = status;
    this.detail = detail;
  }
}

// Endpoint yang TIDAK boleh trigger auto-logout (public endpoints)
const PUBLIC_PATHS = ["/share/", "/auth/login", "/auth/register"];

function isPublicPath(url?: string): boolean {
  if (!url) return false;
  return PUBLIC_PATHS.some((p) => url.includes(p));
}

api.interceptors.response.use(
  (res) => res,
  (error: AxiosError<any>) => {
    const url = error.config?.url;

    // Hanya auto-logout kalau:
    // 1. Status 401
    // 2. Bukan public endpoint
    // 3. Ada token (artinya user sedang login)
    if (
      error.response?.status === 401 &&
      !isPublicPath(url) &&
      getToken()
    ) {
      clearToken();
      if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("yudit:unauthorized"));
      }
    }

    const detail =
      error.response?.data?.detail ||
      error.response?.data?.message ||
      error.message ||
      "Terjadi kesalahan";

    return Promise.reject(
      new ApiError(error.response?.status || 0, String(detail)),
    );
  },
);

export function getApiUrl(path: string): string {
  if (path.startsWith("http")) return path;
  return `${API_URL}${path.startsWith("/") ? "" : "/"}${path}`;
}

// src/lib/api/files.ts
import { api } from "./client";

// =====================================================
// TYPES (mirror backend)
// =====================================================
export interface ApiFile {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  mime_type: string | null;
  size: number;
  folder_id: string | null;
  folder_name: string | null;
  drive_account_id: string;
  remote_file_id: string;
  monthly_folder_id: string | null;
  category: string | null;
  category_confidence: number;
  file_type: string | null;
  year: number;
  month: number;
  day: number;
  is_public: boolean;
  share_token: string | null;
  share_expires_at: string | null;
  share_views: number;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

export interface FileListParams {
  folder_id?: string;
  category?: string;
  search?: string;
  year?: number;
  month?: number;
  day?: number;
  date_from?: string;
  date_to?: string;
  file_type?: string;
  sort_by?: "created_at" | "name" | "size" | "year" | "month";
  sort_order?: "asc" | "desc";
}

export interface StatsResponse {
  total_files: number;
  total_size: number;
  by_category: Record<string, number>;
  by_month: Record<string, number>;
  by_year: Record<string, number>;
  by_day: Record<string, number>;
  by_type: Record<string, number>;
}

export interface CategoryStatsResponse {
  categories: string[];
  counts: Record<string, number>;
}

export interface MonthlyFolder {
  id: string;
  folder_name: string;
  remote_folder_id: string;
  year: number;
  month: number;
  drive_account_id: string;
  drive_accounts?: { google_email: string };
}

export interface UploadResponse {
  message: string;
  file: ApiFile;
  stored_in: string;
  monthly_folder: string;
  category: string | null;
  file_type: string | null;
}

// =====================================================
// LIST
// =====================================================
export async function listFiles(params: FileListParams = {}): Promise<ApiFile[]> {
  const clean: Record<string, string | number> = {};
  Object.entries(params).forEach(([k, v]) => {
    if (v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== "") clean[k] = v as string | number;
  });
  const { data } = await api.get<ApiFile[]>("/files/", { params: clean });
  return data;
}

// =====================================================
// STATS
// =====================================================
export async function getStats(year?: number): Promise<StatsResponse> {
  const { data } = await api.get<StatsResponse>("/files/stats", {
    params: year ? { year } : {},
  });
  return data;
}

export async function getCategoryStats(): Promise<CategoryStatsResponse> {
  const { data } = await api.get<CategoryStatsResponse>("/files/categories");
  return data;
}

// =====================================================
// MONTHLY FOLDERS
// =====================================================
export async function listMonthlyFolders(): Promise<MonthlyFolder[]> {
  const { data } = await api.get<MonthlyFolder[]>("/files/monthly-folders");
  return data;
}

// =====================================================
// UPLOAD
// =====================================================
export async function uploadFile(
  file: File,
  folderId?: string | null,
  onProgress?: (pct: number) => void,
): Promise<UploadResponse> {
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append("file", file);
  if (folderId) form.append("folder_id", folderId);

  const { data } = await api.post<UploadResponse>("/files/upload", form, {
    headers: { "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data" },
    onUploadProgress: (e) => {
      if (onProgress && e.total) {
        onProgress(Math.round((e.loaded / e.total) * 100));
      }
    },
  });
  return data;
}

// =====================================================
// DELETE
// =====================================================
export async function deleteFile(fileId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api.delete(`/files/${fileId}`);
}

export async function bulkDelete(fileIds: string[]): Promise<{
  message: string;
  deleted: number;
  failed: { id: string; error: string }[];
}> {
  const { data } = await api.post("/files/bulk-delete", { file_ids: fileIds });
  return data;
}

// =====================================================
// UPDATE CATEGORY
// =====================================================
export async function updateCategory(
  fileId: string,
  category: string,
): Promise<{ message: string; file: ApiFile }> {
  const { data } = await api.patch(`/files/${fileId}/category`, { category });
  return data;
}

export async function bulkUpdateCategory(
  fileIds: string[],
  category: string,
): Promise<{ message: string; updated: number }> {
  const { data } = await api.post("/files/bulk-category", {
    file_ids: fileIds,
    category,
  });
  return data;
}

export async function reclassify(
  fileId: string,
): Promise<{ message: string; category: string; confidence: number; file: ApiFile }> {
  const { data } = await api.post(`/files/${fileId}/reclassify`);
  return data;
}

// =====================================================
// URL HELPERS (untuk <img>, <video>, download)
// =====================================================
import { getApiUrl, getToken } from "./client";

/**
 * Karena endpoint preview/download butuh Authorization header,
 * kita tidak bisa pakai URL langsung di <img src>. Solusinya:
 * - Fetch sebagai blob via axios → object URL
 * - Atau gunakan token di query (TIDAK direkomendasikan)
 *
 * Di sini kita sediakan helper untuk fetch blob.
 */
export async function fetchFileBlob(
  fileId: string,
  mode: "preview" | "download" = "preview",
): Promise<Blob> {
  const res = await api.get(`/files/${fileId}/${mode}`, {
    responseType: "blob",
  });
  return res.data;
}

export function getFilePreviewUrl(fileId: string): string {
  return getApiUrl(`/files/${fileId}/preview`);
}

export function getFileDownloadUrl(fileId: string): string {
  return getApiUrl(`/files/${fileId}/download`);
}
